An account that\nstill has transactions is never removed, and no transaction is changed or removed to make room.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validate `<code>` with the code rule: 1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`. An invalid code\n  exits 3.\n- Read the account. An absent code exits 4 and writes nothing.\n- Read the transactions. If any transaction names the account, exit 3 and change nothing.\n- Otherwise delete the account row inside the command's transaction, commit, then write the header\n  `deleted` and the code.\n- On any failure, write nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to refuse deletion while the account is in use - supplies: none - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-005` — write: delete the account row - supplies: `account` - returns: `deleted`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-delete-removes ===\nIntent: Deleting an account with no transactions exits 0, writes deleted Post\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Post a new cash transaction through `ledger txn post`, assigning the next transaction id. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-ACCOUNT-ADD.md, FEATURE-LOAD-CATEGORIES.md, FEATURE-NONNEGATIVE-BALANCE.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-CODE.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-TEXT.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-AMOUNT.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-DATE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn post |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, categories.get, transactions.list, transactions.create |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]` records one receipt or payment\nagainst an existing account and category. The command assigns the transaction id; the caller\nnever supplies it.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validate the account and category codes, the amount, the date, and the memo. The memo is optional\n  and holds 0 to 60 characters with no comma or newline. Any violation exits 3.\n- Read the account and the category. An unknown account or category exits 3.\n- Read the largest existing `txn_id`. The new id is one more than that, or `1` when the table is\n  empty.\n- Insert the row, check that no account balance is below zero, then commit.\n- On success, write the header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the new row. Amounts\n  are written with exactly two decimal places.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-002` — read: confirm the category exists - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`, `description`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to find the largest `txn_id` and check balances - supplies: none - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-001` — write: insert the new transaction - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-round-trip ===\nIntent: Posting a transaction exits 0, writes the header and the db.accounts.create(account=account_code, name=\"Cash One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=category_code, description=\"Sales receipts\")\namount_text = \"45.50\"\namount_cents = 4550\ndate_value memo_value\n)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert exit_code == 0\nlines = out.splitlines()\nassert lines[0] == \"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\"\nnew_id = int(lines[1].split(\",\")[0])\nwith ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TXN-AMEND.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Amend\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Change the amount of one transaction through `ledger txn amend <id> <amount>`. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-TXN-GET.md, FEATURE-NONNEGATIVE-BALANCE.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-AMOUNT.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn amend |\n| Consumes    | transactions.get, transactions.list, transactions.update |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn amend <id> <amount>` replaces the amount of one transaction. No other field changes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validate the amount with the amount rule. A violation exits 3.\n- Read the transaction. An absent id exits 4 and writes nothing.\n- Update only the `amount` field inside the command's transaction.\n- Check that no account balance is below zero after the change. A violation exits 3 and rolls back.\n- On success, commit and write the header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the updated\n  row.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — Post\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Post a new cash transaction through `ledger txn post`, assigning the next transaction id. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-ACCOUNT-ADD.md, FEATURE-LOAD-CATEGORIES.md, FEATURE-NONNEGATIVE-BALANCE.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-CODE.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-TEXT.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-AMOUNT.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-DATE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn post |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, categories.get, transactions.list, transactions.create |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]` records one receipt or payment\nagainst an existing account and category. The command assigns the transaction id; the caller\nnever supplies it.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validate the account and category codes, the amount, the date, and the memo. The memo is optional\n  and holds 0 to 60 characters with no comma or newline. Any violation exits 3.\n- Read the account and the category. An unknown account or category exits 3.\n- Read the largest existing `txn_id`. The new id is one more than that, or `1` when the table is\n  empty.\n- Insert the row, check that no account balance is below zero, then commit.\n- On success, write the header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the new row. Amounts\n  are written with exactly two decimal places.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: confirm the account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-002` — read: confirm the category exists - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`, `description`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to find the largest `txn_id` and check balances - supplies: none - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-001` — write: insert the new transaction - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-round-trip ===\nIntent: Posting a transaction exits 0, writes the header and the new row, and persists the row with integer cents.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ndsn = Consumes    | transactions.get |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn get <id>` reads a single transaction and writes it as a report row. `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the one transaction row.\n  Amounts are written with exactly two decimal places.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: get a = 4550\ntxn_id_value = 1\nwith db_session(dsn) as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=account_code, name=\"Cash One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=category_code, description=\"Sales )\ntxn_id_text = \"1\"\n\nexit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"get\", txn_id_text)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert exit_code == 0\nlines = out.splitlines()\nassert lines[0] == ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TXN-AMEND.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Amend\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Change the amount of one transaction through `ledger txn amend <id> <amount>`. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-TXN-GET.md, FEATURE-NONNEGATIVE-BALANCE.md, FEATURE-VALIDATE-AMOUNT.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn amend |\n| Consumes    | transactions.get, transactions.list, transactions.update |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn amend <id> <amount>` replaces the amount of one transaction. No other field changes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validate the amount with the amount rule. A violation exits 3.\n- Read the transaction. An absent id exits 4 and writes nothing.\n- Update only the `amount` field inside the command's transaction.\n- Check that no account balance is below zero after the change. A violation exits 3 and rolls back.\n- On success, commit and write the header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the updated\n  row.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: confirm the transaction exists - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to check balances after the change - supplies: none - returns: `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-004` — write: update the amount - supplies: `txn_id`, `amount` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-amend-changes-only-amount ===\nIntent: Amending a transaction exits 0, stores the new amount, and leaves date, account, category, and memo unchanged.\n\nimport os\nimport ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Delete\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Delete one transaction through `ledger txn delete <id>`. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-TXN-GET.md, FEATURE-TXN-POST.md, FEATURE-NONNEGATIVE-BALANCE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn delete |\n| Consumes    | transactions.get, transactions.list, transactions.delete |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn delete <id>` removes one transaction. A deleted id becomes available again: the next\npost takes the largest remaining id plus one.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Read the transaction. An absent id exits 4 and writes nothing.\n- Delete the row inside the command's transaction.\n- Check that no account balance is below zero after the deletion. A violation exits 3 and rolls back.\n- On success, commit and write the header `deleted` and the id.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: confirm the transaction exists - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to check balances after the deletion - supplies: none - returns: `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-005` — write: delete the transaction row - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `deleted`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-delete-removes-row ===\nIntent: Deleting a transaction exits 0, writes deleted then the id, db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=account_code, name=\"Cash One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=category_code, description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=txn_id_value, date=date_value, account=account_code, category=category_code,\n        amount=seeded_cents, memo=\"seed\",\n    )\ntxn_id_text = \"1\"\n\nexit_code, out, err = not in [row.txn_id for row in db.transactions.list()]\n=== END AC txn-delete-removes-row ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-rejects-negative-balance ===\nIntent: Deleting a credit that would leave an account
